To aid in those kinds of efforts, we present Kine2Go - a dataset with 800 diverse gait kinematics trajectory motion data for the Unitree Go2 robot, derived from 40 distinct policies. Our pipeline accepts data from various quadruped morphologies and translates them to a Go2-compatible format. Then we use Reinforcement Learning to train policies following a given motion, and finally we gather data from those policies, which grants robust, perturbed kinematic data with corresponding motor-level actions.

</details>


### [108] [CADET: Physics-Grounded Causal Auditing and Training-Free Deconfounding of End-to-End Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14438)
*Zikun Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CADET是一个无需重新训练即可审计、基准测试和修复已部署端到端自动驾驶规划器中虚假因果依赖关系的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶规划器通过模仿学习训练时，容易产生统计捷径，即依赖与专家动作仅共现的场景元素（如路边物体、建筑立面）而非真正的因果变量做决策，这种因果混淆在长尾场景中会悄然损害可靠性，且难以通过现有开环指标检测。

Method: 提出CADET框架，无需参数更新即可在预训练的端到端规划器上审计、基准测试和修复虚假依赖关系。

Result: CADET能够在不重新训练模型的情况下，有效检测和修复规划器对虚假线索的依赖。

Conclusion: CADET为已部署的端到端自动驾驶规划器提供了一种无需重新训练即可解决因果混淆问题的有效方案，弥补了现有方法需要重新训练大模型且无法审计已部署规划器的不足。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous-driving planners trained by imitation are prone to statistical shortcuts: they associate scene elements that merely co-occur with expert actions (a roadside object, a building facade) with driving decisions, rather than the variables that causally determine them. Such causal confusion silently compromises reliability in long-tail scenarios, and it is difficult to detect, because prevailing open-loop metrics (L2 displacement and collision rate) are dominated by ego status and do not indicate whether a planner depends on spurious cues. Existing remedies based on causal-intervention training require retraining large models and cannot audit a planner that is already deployed. We present CADET, a training-free framework that audits, benchmarks, and repairs spurious reliance in pretrained E2E planners without any parameter update.

</details>


### [109] [AERMANI-PLACE: Language Guided Object Placement with Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14531)
*Sarthak Mishra,Ritama Sanyal,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AERMANI-PLACE框架，通过自然语言指令和图像编辑模型生成视觉标记，引导空中机械臂完成物体放置任务，在仿真和真实平台上分别达到87%和72%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中机械臂的物体放置系统需要用户以公制坐标明确指定放置位置，这种接口不直观且难以实际部署；而人类通常通过语言和手势的组合来沟通空间目标，受此启发，提出更自然的语言引导放置方式。

Method: 输入场景图像和自然语言指令→图像编辑模型生成带有视觉标记的修改场景→利用深度观测将标记映射到物理环境中的公制放置点→生成并执行放置轨迹。

Result: 在100个语言引导放置任务测试集上平均成功率87%，在真实空中机械臂平台上平均成功率72%。

Conclusion: 所提方法能可靠地从语言指令推断放置位置，并有效迁移到真实空中操控场景。

Abstract: Object placement is a fundamental component of aerial manipulation tasks, yet existing systems typically require the desired placement position to be specified explicitly in metric coordinates. Such interfaces are not intuitive and require users to reason about coordinate frames and scene geometry, making them difficult to use in practical deployments. In contrast, humans often communicate spatial goals through a combination of language and pointing gestures. Inspired by this observation, we present AERMANI-PLACE, a framework for language-guided object placement with aerial manipulators. Given a scene image and a natural language instruction, an image editing model generates a modified version of the scene containing a visual marker that indicates where the object should be placed. This marker is then grounded into the physical environment using depth observations to recover a metric place point, after which a placement trajectory is generated and executed by the aerial manipulator. We evaluate the proposed approach on a test set of 100 language-guided placement tasks and demonstrate successful execution on a real aerial manipulation platform. Experimental results show that the proposed method reliably infers placement locations from language instructions with an average success rate of 87\% on the test-set and transfers effectively to real-world aerial manipulation with an average success rate of 72\%.
  Video: https://youtu.be/SgwwgLBsv0g

</details>


### [110] [Spatially Conditioned Diffusion Policy: Learning Precise and Robust Manipulation with a Single RGB Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14535)
*Seoyoon Kim,Kanghyun Kim,Dongwoo Ko,Yeong Jin Heo,Min Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间条件扩散策略（SCDP），利用末端执行器轨迹作为视觉注意力锚点，在单摄像头设置下实现了精确且鲁棒的模仿学习，性能媲美多摄像头方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模仿学习系统普遍依赖多摄像头（尤其是腕部摄像头），但单全局视角下策略难以捕捉细粒度交互细节和识别任务相关区域，亟需一种不依赖局部腕部视图的解决方案。

Method: 基于扩散策略框架，包含两个核心模块：(i) 多尺度特征图视觉编码器，捕获全局上下文与细粒度视觉特征；(ii) 空间条件模块，在扩散循环中沿中间末端执行器轨迹采样逐点特征。

Result: 仿真实验一致优于强单视角基线，性能与多摄像头基线相当；真实实验展示了精确操控能力和对视觉干扰物的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCDP验证了单摄像头模仿学习的可行性，通过轨迹引导的视觉注意力机制有效替代了多摄像头设置，为简化模仿学习系统硬件配置提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent visual imitation learning systems have widely adopted multi-camera setups with wrist-mounted cameras as the de facto standard. However, manipulation from a single global view remains challenging, as the policy should capture fine-grained interaction details and identify task-relevant regions without local wrist views. To address this challenge, we present Spatially Conditioned Diffusion Policy (SCDP), a diffusion-based visuomotor policy that achieves precise and robust manipulation in a single-camera setting. Our key idea is that end-effector trajectories can serve as visual attention anchors that reflect task-relevant regions. Building on this idea, SCDP consists of two key components: (i) a visual encoder that produces multi-scale feature maps to capture both broader context and fine-grained visual features, and (ii) a spatial conditioning module that samples point-wise features along intermediate end-effector trajectories in the diffusion loop. Extensive simulation experiments show that SCDP consistently outperforms strong single-view baselines and achieves performance comparable to multi-camera baselines. Real-world experiments further demonstrate precise manipulation and robustness to visual distractors, highlighting the potential of single-camera imitation learning.

</details>


### [111] [TRACE: Trajectory-Routed Causal Memory for Delayed-Evidence Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14551)
*Zihao Li,Ranpeng Qiu,Yincong Chen,Guoqiang Ren,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE是一种基于轨迹路径签名的记忆框架，通过存储和检索早期视觉证据来解决机器人操作中的延迟证据问题，在不需要修改策略主干的情况下提升长期任务的决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自主操作中常面临"延迟证据"任务——早期视觉线索在后续决策点消失，导致视觉相似但需要不同动作的观测出现，当前观测不足以作为控制状态，需要记忆机制来保留早期关键信息。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，使用路径签名（path signatures）作为轨迹条件键，将任务相关的视觉和机器人状态证据存储在固定大小的潜在记忆中。路径签名是紧凑的、对顺序敏感的执行轨迹特征，用于写入和检索可见时存储的证据，不存储视觉线索本身。

Result: 在真实世界的长时程操作任务中，TRACE在分支选择和任务成功率上优于短期历史和循环记忆等基线方法。

Conclusion: TRACE通过轻量适配器附加到已有策略上，无需改变策略主干、动作头或模仿学习目标，有效解决了视觉模糊分支点的决策问题。

Abstract: Robots under autonomous operation may require decisions based on evidence that is no longer visible. We study \emph{delayed-evidence} tasks, where an early cue disappears before a later decision point, so visually similar observations can require different actions. In these settings, the current observation is not a sufficient state for control. We introduce TRAjectory-routed Causal Evidence (TRACE), a memory framework for visuomotor imitation policies. TRACE stores task-relevant visual and robot-state evidence, such as object identity, target choice, or route-dependent state, in a fixed-size latent memory that remains bounded over long episodes. Instead of indexing memory by raw time or manually provided task labels, TRACE uses \emph{path signatures}: compact, order-sensitive features of the executed robot-state trajectory. These signatures do not store the visual cue itself; rather, they provide trajectory-conditioned keys for writing and retrieving the evidence stored when the cue was visible. When the robot later reaches an ambiguous observation, the policy conditions on TRACE memory to recover the missing context and choose the correct branch. TRACE attaches through lightweight adapters to policies, without changing the policy backbone, action head, or imitation objective. Across real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks with visually ambiguous branch points, TRACE improves branch selection and task success over alternative baselines, including short-history and recurrent memory. Project page: https://jeong-zju.github.io/trace

</details>


### [112] [ORCA: A Platform for Open-Source Dexterity Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14561)
*Francesco Capuano,Maximilian Eberlein,Fabrice Bourquin,Clemens Claudio Christoph*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ORCA开源研究栈，旨在将灵巧手操控（特别是拟人手）作为机器人学习的一等研究领域。该栈统一了底层控制、仿真、遥操作（支持消费级VR头显）和手部重定向，并原生集成LeRobot等流行框架，为灵巧手研究提供与常规机器人学习相同的数据、训练和评估流程。作者通过手内重定向任务的完整端到端工作流（VR遥操作采集示范→LeRobot训练策略→可复现评估）验证了系统有效性，并开源了全部代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手研究（尤其是拟人手）虽然更接近人类手部结构且能从人类视频学习，但其软件生态碎片化——控制、仿真、遥操作和重定向代码分散在不同代码库中，且与主流机器人学习框架脱节，阻碍了灵巧操控的学习研究。

Method: 构建ORCA开源研究栈：1) 统一底层控制、仿真、多平台遥操作（消费级VR头显等）和手部重定向接口；2) 原生集成LeRobot等机器人学习框架；3) 通过VR头显采集手内重定向任务专家示范，利用LeRobot训练自主策略，在可复现可观测的平台上评估学习到的策略。

Result: 实现了一个完整的端到端工作流：从消费级VR遥操作采集灵巧手专家示范数据，到利用LeRobot训练自主策略，再到完全可复现和可观测的评估环境。整个栈作为共享可复现的基础设施开源。

Conclusion: ORCA栈解决了灵巧手研究软件生态碎片化问题，将灵巧操控提升为与常规机器人学习同等地位的一等研究领域，为灵巧手研究者提供了统一、可复现的研究基础。

Abstract: Robotics manipulation research increasingly focuses on two-finger parallel grippers for their effectiveness, affordability, and ease of teleoperation. Grippers are nonetheless limited by their form factor, often requiring bimanual setups even for simple reorientation tasks. Anthropomorphic hands are a more natural platform for dexterous robot learning -- closer to the human hand, and capable of learning from human video -- yet they remain hard to use in learning research: even where open and accessible hand hardware exists, the software for control, simulation, teleoperation, and retargeting is scattered in one-off code bases, and largely disconnected from the robot-learning ecosystem. In this work, we introduce the \orca~learning stack, an open-source research stack for dexterity as a first-class robot learning domain. Our \orca~stack unifies low-level control, simulation, teleoperation from a range of consumer platforms, and hand retargeting, behind a single interface, and integrates natively with popular robot-learning frameworks such as \lerobot, so dexterous hand researchers can leverage the same data, training, and evaluation pipelines used for non-dexterous robot learning. We demonstrate a complete end-to-end workflow, collecting expert demonstrations of an in-hand reorientation task by teleoperation with a consumer-grade VR headset, training an autonomous policy with \lerobot, and evaluating the learned policy in a fully reproducible and observable setup. We open-source the entire stack as a shared, reproducible foundation for dexterous-manipulation research.

</details>


### [113] [Sensitivity Shaping for Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14585)
*Hongzhan Yu,Chenghao Li,Ruipeng Zhang,Henrik Christensen,Sicun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出支持条件控制敏感性正则化方法，通过学习对控制输入变化敏感的生成动力学模型，改进策略引发的分布外（OOD）过渡检测。该方法在训练高支持区域保持控制诱导变异，同时限制弱经验支持下的不稳定外推。在视觉避障、操作和真实机器人导航实验中，展示了改进的OOD检测和更安全的闭环规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将学习到的动力学模型视为固定并附加事后支持代理，但当动力学模型对关键动作选择局部不敏感时，这些代理可能失效：不受支持的控制动作可能产生与演示过渡相似的潜在预测，从而抑制OOD信号，尽管存在较大的真实预测误差。

Method: 提出支持条件控制敏感性正则化（support-conditioned control-sensitivity regularization），在学习到的动力学模型的高支持训练区域中，促进对控制输入变化的敏感局部响应。

Result: 在视觉避障、操作任务和真实机器人导航实验中，该方法实现了改进的OOD检测性能和更安全的闭环规划。

Conclusion: 通过让动力学模型对控制输入变化保持敏感，同时限制弱支持区域的不稳定外推，可以显著提升策略诱导的OOD过渡检测能力，进而保障机器人系统部署的安全性。

Abstract: Generative dynamics models enable planning in challenging robotic systems, but safe deployment requires reliably detecting policy-induced out-of-distribution (OOD) transitions. Existing methods typically treat the learned dynamics as fixed and attach post hoc support surrogates. We show that these surrogates can fail when the dynamics are locally insensitive to critical action choices: unsupported control actions may produce latent predictions that resemble demonstrated transitions, suppressing OOD signals despite large true predictive errors. To address this, we introduce support-conditioned control-sensitivity regularization, which promotes sensitive local response to control input changes in learned dynamics in high-support training regions. This preserves control-induced variation while limiting unstable extrapolation due to weak empirical support. Experiments in vision-based obstacle avoidance, manipulation, and real-robot navigation show improved OOD detection and safer closed-loop planning.

</details>


### [114] [What Robots Do Matters More Than What They Look Like: Task Context Shapes Trust in Educational HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14602)
*Anna-Maria Velentza,Konstantina Nikou,Anne-Gwenn Bosser,Nikolaos Fachantidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过视频实验（N=81）探究机器人外观与任务类型对信任的影响，发现任务类型（教学指导>教学活动>个人信息询问）显著影响信任，而机器人外观无显著主效应，表明人机信任更多由任务情境而非物理外观塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交辅助机器人在教育和信息传递场景中应用日益广泛，但单一机器人外观是否适用于不同交互任务尚不明确，信任是否主要取决于情境因素也未得到充分探究。

Method: 采用被试内视频实验设计（N=81），参与者评估三种不同外观的机器人在三种教育相关任务（教学、程序性指导、个人信息讨论）中的表现，通过重复测量分析检验机器人外观与任务类型对信任的影响。

Result: 任务类型对信任有显著主效应（程序性指导信任最高，教学活动次之，个人信息请求时显著降低）；机器人外观无显著主效应；外观与任务的交互效应边缘不显著。

Conclusion: 人机交互中的信任更多由任务情境而非物理外观决定；在教育环境中部署机器人时应根据任务目标对齐角色与行为，而非仅依赖拟人化设计。

Abstract: Socially assistive robots (SARs) are increasingly deployed in educational and information-sharing contexts, supported by advances in large language models that enable fluent real-time interaction. Despite the growing diversity of robot embodiments, it remains unclear whether a single robot appearance is appropriate across different interaction tasks or whether trust depends primarily on contextual factors. In this study, we examine how robot appearance and task type jointly influence trust in robots. Using a within-subjects video-based experiment (N = 81), participants evaluated three robots with distinct appearances while performing three educationally relevant tasks: teaching, procedural instruction, and personal-information discussion. Results from repeated-measures analyses show a strong main effect of task on trust, with participants reporting the highest trust during instructional guidance, moderate trust during teaching activities, and significantly lower trust when robots requested personal information. In contrast, robot appearance showed no significant main effect, and the interaction between appearance and task was marginal. These findings suggest that trust in human-robot interaction is shaped more strongly by task context than by physical embodiment alone. By focusing on future educators as end users, this work contributes empirical evidence toward task-aware robot deployment in educational environments and highlights the importance of aligning robot roles and behaviors with interaction goals rather than relying solely on anthropomorphic design.

</details>


### [115] [Impedance MPC with Disturbance Estimation for Dexterous Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14606)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于灵巧手的阻抗模型预测控制（Impedance MPC）框架，通过前馈补偿将各种腱驱动传动简化为常系数双积分器，实现离线预计算QP成本逆矩阵，在500 Hz下运行10步滚动时域优化，同时硬约束接触力、驱动极限和加加速度。在水压驱动手指上，该方法的RMS精度（0.5 mrad）、稳态误差（0.1 mrad）和峰值偏差（6.6 mrad）分别比经典阻抗控制提升183倍、1500倍和23倍，并在16自由度LEAP Hand仿真中验证了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手需要同时跟踪精确的指尖轨迹并保持安全柔顺的接触，这两个目标对于任何固定增益控制器而言是相互矛盾的。现有控制方法难以同时满足高精度轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺接触的要求，因此需要一种新的控制框架来协调这两者之间的张力。

Method: 提出一种执行器无关的Impedance MPC框架，采用恒定的A_d偏移消除架构（继承自pHRI的稳定性、递归可行性和输入状态稳定性保证）。通过代数前馈将各种腱驱动传动（液压、线缆、气动、扭绳、串联弹性）统一简化为常系数双积分器，使得QP成本逆矩阵可离线预计算。采用10步滚动时域QP，在500 Hz下运行，同时硬约束接触力（ISO/TS 15066标准）、驱动极限和加加速度。使用仅编码器的增广卡尔曼扰动状态来消除任意恒定接触负载下的稳态误差。

Result: 在水压驱动手指上：RMS 0.5 mrad，稳态误差0.1 mrad，1.5 Nm接触下峰值偏差6.6 mrad，分别比经典阻抗控制提升183倍、1500倍和23倍。首次动作刚度（随更新率从18变化到323 Nm/rad）得到独立验证。在16自由度LEAP Hand MuJoCo仿真中，2.5 N抓取负载扰动可在0.7秒内恢复。

Conclusion: 提出的Impedance MPC框架能在保持安全柔顺接触的同时实现高精度轨迹跟踪，性能显著优于经典阻抗控制，且具有执行器无关性和良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Dexterous hands must simultaneously track precise finger trajectories and maintain safe, compliant contact -- objectives in tension for any fixed-gain controller. We present an actuator-agnostic Impedance Model Predictive Control (Impedance MPC) framework for dexterous fingers, instantiating the constant-$A_d$ offset-free architecture established for physical human-robot interaction (pHRI); its stability, recursive-feasibility, and input-to-state-stability guarantees are inherited by preserving the architectural assumptions. An algebraic feedforward reduces the tendon transmission -- hydraulic, cable, pneumatic, twisted-string, or series-elastic -- to a constant-coefficient double integrator, so the QP cost inverse is precomputed offline and a 10-step receding-horizon quadratic program runs at 500\,Hz while enforcing hard constraints on contact force (ISO/TS 15066), actuation limits, and jerk. An encoder-only augmented-Kalman disturbance state drives steady-state error to zero under any constant contact load. On a hydraulically actuated finger -- the worked example platform, adding pressure and cavitation constraints -- the 500\,Hz Kalman MPC attains 0.5\,mrad RMS, 0.1\,mrad steady-state, and 6.6\,mrad peak deflection under 1.5\,Nm contact: 183$\times$, 1500$\times$, and 23$\times$ better than classical impedance. The realized first-move stiffness (18$\to$323\,Nm/rad with update rate) is independently verified. The architecture scales to a 16-DOF LEAP Hand MuJoCo simulation, recovering from 2.5\,N grasp-load disturbances within 0.7\,s.

</details>


### [116] [Safe Reinforcement Learning of Autonomous Highway Driving: A Unified Framework for Safety and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14609)
*Chufei Yan,Zhihao Cui,Yiyan Lv,Taojie Chen,Ning Bian,Yulei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合安全距离(SD)、奖励机(RM)和混合专家(MoE)的统一安全强化学习框架(MoE-RM-SRL)，用于自动驾驶车辆，在训练和部署阶段同时保证安全性和高效性，并在CARLA和6-DoF驾驶员在环VR平台上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在自动驾驶决策中具有潜力，但其试错式学习特性导致训练阶段难以保证安全性，且部署阶段难以同时兼顾安全与效率。

Method: 提出MoE-RM-SRL框架：(1) 部署阶段，SD与RM联合构建规则感知奖励，编码高速公路交通规则和阶段目标；(2) 训练阶段，引入稀疏门控MoE层（最多11个DQN专家），基于SD的门控规则激活最小专家集，用于车道保持和车道变换，缓解异构控制器切换带来的不稳定性。

Result: 在CARLA仿真器和6-DoF驾驶员在环VR平台上的随机双车道交通实验中，MoE-RM-SRL在安全性和效率上显著优于现有基线方法，并能够自然扩展到多车道驾驶、匝道合流及出口等场景。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了DRL在AV中安全保证与效率兼顾的挑战，具有良好泛化性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) offers a compelling route to decision-making for advanced autonomous vehicles (AVs), yet its trial-and-error nature makes it difficult to guarantee safety during training and to achieve both safety and efficiency at deployment. We propose a unified safe reinforcement learning (SRL) framework that integrates safe distance (SD), reward machines (RM), and mixture-of-experts (MoE), termed MoE-RM-SRL. For deployment, SD and RM jointly shape a rule-aware reward that encodes highway traffic regulations and stage-wise objectives, enabling safe and reliable behavior without sacrificing efficiency. For training, we introduce a sparsely gated MoE layer comprising up to 11 deep Q-networks (DQNs); an SD-based gating rule activates a minimal set of experts for lane-keeping and lane-changing, mitigating the instability, discontinuities, and impulsive transients commonly induced by switching between heterogeneous controllers (e.g., MPC/rule-based modules and learned policies). We implement the proposed architecture in CARLA and integrate it with a 6-DoF driver-in-the-loop virtual-reality (DiL-VR) platform. Experiments in stochastic two-lane traffic show that MoE-RM-SRL substantially improves safety and efficiency over state-of-the-art baselines, and the framework naturally extends to multi-lane driving as well as on-ramp merging and exiting scenarios.

</details>


### [117] [Whole-Body Impedance Model Predictive Control for Safe Physical Human--Robot Interaction on Floating-Base Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14617)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于阻抗MPC的三层全身控制架构，用于浮动基座机器人在刚性接触约束下与人交互时的平衡控制。该架构通过质心MPC、优先级WBC和残差空间QP三层协同，结合接触一致反馈线性化和协方差膨胀协议，实现了对持续人机交互力的零稳态误差跟踪，并在仿真中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮动基座机器人在刚性接触约束下需要平衡控制，同时安全地与人类交互。现有WBC框架要么将全部关节空间分配给 locomotion，要么依赖固定增益阻抗反馈，在持续的物理人机交互力作用下会产生稳态误差。

Method: 提出三层架构：1) 质心MPC在500ms时域内规划接触力；2) 优先级驱动的WBC层通过接触一致零空间投影将平衡解析为关节力矩；3) 残差零空间由滚动时域QP控制，利用卡尔曼增强状态预测和抑制pHRI扰动。采用接触一致反馈线性化将臂端执行器简化为双积分器，实现在线QP成本预计算和≥1kHz运行。协方差膨胀协议保证接触模式切换时扰动估计的连续性，实现零稳态误差。

Result: 在17-DOF双足机器人和Unitree G1人形机器人上的仿真验证了所提架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过阻抗等价定理证明，无限时域极限下该方法可恢复经典任务空间阻抗定律，其有效质量、阻尼和刚度可随姿态和接触构型自适应调整。该方法实现了对恒定有界pHRI负载的零稳态误差跟踪。

Abstract: Floating-base robots must balance under rigid contact constraints while interacting safely with humans. Existing whole-body control~(WBC) frameworks allocate the full joint space to locomotion or rely on fixed-gain impedance feedback that accumulates steady-state error under sustained physical human--robot interaction~(pHRI) forces. This paper extends the authors' fixed-base two-layer Impedance MPC to floating-base platforms through a three-level architecture: a centroidal MPC plans contact forces over a 500\,ms horizon; a priority-driven WBC layer resolves balance into joint torques through contact-consistent null-space projection; and the residual null space is governed by a receding-horizon quadratic program~(QP) that predicts and rejects pHRI disturbances using a Kalman-augmented state. A contact-consistent feedback linearization reduces the arm end-effector plant to a double integrator with a \emph{constant} state matrix within each contact mode, enabling offline precomputation of the QP cost and ${\geq}1$\,kHz operation. A covariance-inflation protocol preserves the disturbance estimate across contact-mode switches, guaranteeing zero steady-state error under bounded constant pHRI loads, and an Impedance Equivalence Theorem shows the infinite-horizon limit recovers a classical task-space impedance law whose effective mass, damping, and stiffness adapt to posture and contact configuration. Simulations on a 17-DOF biped and the Unitree G1 humanoid validate the design.

</details>


### [118] [EgoGuide: Egocentric Guidance for Efficient Robot-Free Demonstration Collection and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14665)
*Yue Xu,Mingtao Nie,Tianle Li,Hong Li,Yibo Luo,Siyuan Huang,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoGuide提出了一种结合腕部和头部/自我中心观测的机器人数据收集接口，通过在线视觉-几何质量指导提升数据效率，并采用门控自我中心残差策略增强在视角变化和视觉遮挡下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMI-style数据收集管道存在冗余演示多、缺乏全局场景上下文的问题，导致数据效率低下。

Method: 1) 设计EgoGuide数据收集接口，同步记录腕部和头部/自我中心观测，并引入在线视觉-几何数据质量指导；2) 提出门控自我中心残差策略(Gated Egocentric Residual Policy)，利用头部/自我中心上下文纠正模糊的局部观测，同时保持稳定的腕部视角控制。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，EgoGuide减少了所需的数据集数量，提升了数据效率；残差策略在视觉遮挡下表现出更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过多视角协同观测与数据质量指导，EgoGuide有效解决了数据冗余和上下文缺失问题，为机器人从演示中学习提供了更高效、鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Robot learning from real-world demonstrations is currently constrained by data scaling. Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) provides an efficient robot-free data collection interface, yet current UMI-style pipelines often collect redundant demonstrations and lack global scene context. To improve data efficiency, we present EgoGuide, a collection interface that records synchronized wrist and head/egocentric observations and couples them with online visual-geometric data quality guidance. We also introduce a Gated Egocentric Residual Policy for robust learning from a viewpoint-varying egocentric camera, allowing head/egocentric context to correct ambiguous local observations while preserving stable wrist-view control. Real-world experiments show that EgoGuide reduces the required number of data episodes and improves data efficiency. The residual policy further improves robustness under visual occlusion. Project Page: https://silicx.github.io/EgoGuide

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-Based Transformer Method for Solving the Open Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13682)
*Faezeh Ardali,Mwembezi A. Nyelele,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer编码器-解码器架构的开放车间调度策略，仅使用加工时间矩阵作为输入，在Taillard小规模基准上训练后，无需重新训练即可泛化到40x40至100x100的大规模实例，平均gap为12.89-15.12%，在性能上优于SPT和LPT，与EST竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放车间调度问题在工业和服务场景中广泛存在，但随着作业和机器数量增加，精确方法变得难以处理，经典调度规则和元启发式方法在大规模下需要大量调参才能保持解质量，亟需一种轻量级、可泛化的学习方法。

Method: 采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构，结合多头注意力机制；仅以加工时间矩阵作为输入特征；在Taillard基准实例（4x4、5x5、7x7、10x10）上训练；训练后的策略不经过微调直接应用于更大规模的随机生成实例。

Result: 在Taillard小规模实例上，生成调度的makespan通常在最优已知值的15-30%以内；在40x40至100x100的大规模实例上，平均gap为12.89-15.12%；相比EST保持竞争力（差距在较小范围内），显著优于SPT和LPT。

Conclusion: 在小规模OSSP实例上训练的Transformer策略能够有效泛化到大规模问题，提供了一种特征轻量、基于学习的替代方案，可与经典调度规则竞争。

Abstract: The open shop scheduling problem (OSSP) arises in many industrial and service settings but remains computationally challenging as the number of jobs and machines increases. While exact methods quickly become intractable, classical dispatching rules and metaheuristics may require substantial tuning to maintain solution quality at large scales. This study develops a Transformer-based scheduling policy for OSSP using an encoder-decoder architecture with multi-head attention. The model is trained on Taillard benchmark instances (4x4, 5x5, 7x7, and 10x10) using only the processing-time matrix as input and produces feasible schedules with makespans typically within 15-30% of best-known values. To evaluate scalability, the trained policy is applied without retraining to randomly generated instances from 40x40 to 100x100 and compared against classical dispatching heuristics, including SPT, LPT, MWKR, and EST. Across these large instances, the Transformer achieved average gaps of 12.89-15.12% relative to a standard lower bound. Compared with EST, the Transformer remained competitive, typically within a modest margin, while substantially outperforming SPT and LPT. These results indicate that a Transformer policy trained on small OSSP instances can generalize to substantially larger problems and provide a feature-light, learning-based alternative to classical dispatching rules.

</details>


### [120] [UP-NRPA: User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation for Planning with Large Language Models in Goal-oriented Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13683)
*Hui Wang,Fafa Zhang,Meng Liu,Xiangyu Chen,Chaoxu Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UP-NRPA在线框架，利用大语言模型和用户画像实现对话策略的动态自适应，无需离线强化学习，在协作与非协作对话基准测试中取得显著效果，谈判任务销售列表比提升56.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话策略规划方法难以动态适应多样化的用户特征，依赖模型训练和离线强化学习策略模型，缺乏对用户个性化需求的实时响应能力。

Method: 提出UP-NRPA（基于用户画像的嵌套展开策略自适应）在线框架，利用大语言模型，通过实时用户反馈以及从当前用户画像中映射的个性、偏好和目标，实现对话策略的动态定制，无需离线强化学习。

Result: 在协作与非协作对话基准测试中，UP-NRPA在多个对话任务中达到100%的成功率；在谈判任务中，销售列表比（SL）提升56.41%。

Conclusion: UP-NRPA无需训练机制即可适应多样化用户需求，使对话系统能够根据用户特征进行自适应调整。

Abstract: To address the challenge that current dialogue policy planning methods struggle to dynamically adapt to diverse user characteristics, this paper proposes a User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (UP-NRPA) online framework with Large Language Models. In contrast to conventional approaches dependent on model training and require offline reinforcement learning policy models for user groups, UP-NRPA enables dynamic customization of dialogue strategies through an adaptive mechanism. This is achieved by leveraging real-time user feedback alongside personality, preferences, and objectives mapped from the current user portrait, thereby adapting to user characteristics without offline reinforcement learning. In collaborative and non-collaborative dialogue benchmarks, UP-NRPA demonstrated considerable benefits, achieving an impressive 100% success rate in multiple dialogue tasks. Particularly in negotiation tasks, the sale-to-list ratio (SL) increased by 56.41%. This demonstrates that UP-NRPA can adapt to diverse user needs without requiring a training mechanism, enabling the dialogue system to adapt to user characteristics.

</details>


### [121] [History of the Muddy Children Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13703)
*Hans van Ditmarsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文追溯了“泥孩子谜题”的起源，发现其最早可追溯到两个世纪前的逻辑与文学出版物，并介绍了包括数字、彩色帽子在内的多种变体，以及一个新的自指帽子谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泥孩子谜题对认知逻辑的发展具有重要启发意义，但其最初提出者尚不明确，因此作者希望追溯该谜题的真正起源。

Method: 通过梳理过去两个世纪中逻辑学和文学领域的出版物，追踪泥孩子谜题的起源；并设计了一个涉及自指的新型帽子谜题。

Result: 发现泥孩子谜题的起源可追溯到两个世纪前的文献，存在多种变体（如数字、彩色帽子），并提出了一种新的自指帽子谜题。

Conclusion: 泥孩子谜题历史悠久且影响深远，其变体丰富，自指版本进一步拓展了该类谜题的理论边界。

Abstract: The Muddy Children Puzzle is a puzzle about knowledge and ignorance that has been inspiring for the development of epistemic logic. Who came up with it first? This is unclear. We trace the origin of the Muddy Children Puzzle through logical and literary publications over the past two centuries. The puzzle inspired a numerous variations such as involving numbers or coloured hats. We also present a novel hats puzzle involving self-reference.

</details>


### [122] [Orchestra-o1: Omnimodal Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13707)
*Fan Zhang,Vireo Zhang,Shengju Qian,Haoxuan Li,Hao Wu,Jinyang Wu,Donghao Zhou,Zhihong Zhu,Zheng Lian,Xin Wang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Orchestra-o1框架，通过统一编排机制支持多模态代理协同，可进行模态感知的任务分解、在线子代理特化和并行执行，在OmniGAIA基准上超越第二名10.3%，并引入DA-GRPO训练方法使8B模型达到开源全模态代理最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理编排框架仅限于少数模态，难以泛化到文本、图像、音频、视频等异构模态共存并交互的复杂全模态场景。

Method: 提出Orchestra-o1全模态代理编排框架，包含统一编排机制实现模态感知任务分解、在线子代理特化、并行子任务执行；并引入决策对齐组相对策略优化(DA-GRPO)高效代理强化学习训练方法。

Result: 在OmniGAIA基准上超越第二名方法10.3%准确率；Orchestra-o1-8B在开源全模态代理中取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架可有效应对涉及异构信息源的复杂真实任务，且通过DA-GRPO训练的小模型也能达到开源最优水平。

Abstract: The recent success of agent swarms has shifted the paradigm of large language model (LLM)-based agents from single-agent workflows to multi-agent systems, highlighting the importance of agent orchestration for task decomposition and collaboration. However, existing orchestration frameworks are limited to a narrow set of modalities and struggle to generalize to more complex settings where heterogeneous modalities coexist and interact. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced in omnimodal scenarios, where tasks require the unified understanding and coordination of diverse inputs such as text, image, audio, and video. In this work, we propose Orchestra-o1, an omnimodal agent orchestration framework designed to support efficient agent collaboration across multiple modalities. Orchestra-o1 introduces a unified orchestration mechanism that enables modality-aware task decomposition, online sub-agent specialization, and parallel sub-task execution. This scalable design allows agent systems to effectively tackle complex real-world tasks involving heterogeneous information sources, surpassing the second-best approach by 10.3% accuracy on the OmniGAIA benchmark. Furthermore, we introduce decision-aligned group relative policy optimization (DA-GRPO), an efficient agentic reinforcement learning approach for training Orchestra-o1-8B, which also achieves state-of-the-art performance against all existing open-source omnimodal agents.

</details>


### [123] [Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution Makes Better Deep Researcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13710)
*Hongming Piao,Chi Liu,Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Derek Li,Ying Wei,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合开放式三元进化（HOTE）框架，通过混合模式强化学习实现提议者、求解者和评判者的协同进化，使小模型（8B）在长程深度研究基准上超越静态开源8-32B模型及现有深度研究方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究受限于静态参数化能力，而代理进化仅在可验证任务上有效，两者在开放式研究任务之间存在鸿沟。为弥合这一差距，需要一种能在开放式环境中协同进化多个能力模块的框架。

Method: 提出HOTE框架，包含提议者（Proposer）、求解者（Solver）和评判者（Judge）三个模块，通过混合模式强化学习基于网络规模知识实现三者协同进化，使代理在开放式任务和环境中自主进化。

Result: 在三个长程深度研究基准上的实验表明，经HOTE训练的8B模型超越了最强的静态开源8-32B模型以及现有深度研究训练方法，且训练时间开销更少。消融实验验证了三个模块的进化缺一不可。

Conclusion: HOTE框架有效弥合了深度研究与代理进化之间的鸿沟，验证了混合模式强化学习下多模块协同进化在开放式研究任务中的有效性，为自主进化代理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep research and agent evolution serve as de-facto tasks for AI agents in real-world applications toward artificial general intelligence. The former enables autonomous retrieval and integration of information in open-ended environments to tackle open-ended research tasks, yet it is constrained by the static parametric deep research capabilities of agent systems. The latter allows agents to autonomously interact with the environment to gain experiences that evolve model capabilities. However, its effectiveness has been widely validated only on verifiable tasks with standard answers, leaving a gap with open-ended research tasks. To bridge these two critical tasks, we propose the Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution (HOTE) framework, which leverages hybrid-mode reinforcement learning to facilitate the collaborative evolution of a proposer, solver and judge based on web-scale knowledge, moving toward autonomous evolving agents in open-ended tasks and environments. Extensive experiments on three long-form deep research benchmarks demonstrate that the 8B model trained via HOTE surpasses the strongest static open 8-32B models as well as those trained by state-of-the-art deep research training methods with less time overhead, and further verify that the evolution of all three modules in HOTE is indispensable.

</details>


### [124] [WorkBench Revisited: Workplace Agents Two Years On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13715)
*Olly Styles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 从2024年3月到2026年6月，WorkBench上的最佳智能体任务完成率从43%提升至89%，意外有害行为从26%降至2.5%。能力与安全性正相关而非权衡，部分错误已被完全消除，但前沿模型仍存在导致不可逆伤害的基本错误。开源模型大幅降低了高性能的成本，前沿模型成本相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估前沿智能体在2024年至2026年间在WorkBench基准上的能力与安全性进展，检验能力与安全性之间是否存在权衡关系，并分析开源与闭源模型的成本变化趋势。

Method: 对比GPT-4（2024年3月）和Claude Opus 4.8（2026年6月）在WorkBench上的任务完成率和意外有害行为发生率，分析错误类别消除情况，比较开源与前沿模型的性能成本。

Result: 最佳智能体任务完成率从43%升至89%，意外有害行为从26%降至2.5%。能力与安全性呈正相关。部分错误类别已被完全消除，但前沿模型仍存在导致不可逆伤害的基本错误。开源模型大幅降低了高性能门槛，前沿模型成本保持稳定。

Conclusion: 智能体在能力和安全性上取得了显著进步，两者并非权衡关系；开源模型的崛起使高性能智能体更加可及，但前沿模型仍需解决残余的基本错误以防止不可逆伤害。

Abstract: The best agent on WorkBench in March 2024, GPT-4, completed 43% of tasks and took an unintended harmful action, such as emailing the wrong person, on 26% of them. We re-visit the benchmark in June 2026 and find that the best agent to date, Claude Opus 4.8, completes 89% and takes an unintended harmful action on 2.5%. Aside from this considerable progress in frontier agent performance, three things stand out. First, capability and safety go together on WorkBench rather than trade off, so the models that finish the most tasks also do the least unintended damage. Second, while several classes of error have been totally eliminated, frontier models still make some basic mistakes that occasionally result in irreversible harm, such as sending an email to the wrong person. Third, the rise of open-weight models has drastically lowered costs for a performance level that was previously only accessible to proprietary models, while frontier costs have stayed relatively stable. We release an updated version of the benchmark with data and code quality improvements, new model scores, and analysis of agent progress on WorkBench since 2024.

</details>


### [125] [Refusal Beyond a Single Direction: A Preliminary Comparison of Diff-in-Means and INLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13720)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 比较了两种干预方法（DiM和INLP）在操控安全微调聊天模型拒绝行为上的效果，发现INLP反事实翻转与DiM方向消融效果相当，而零空间投影较弱；限制INLP到主要方向可在保持低困惑度的同时实现可调控制；两类INLP干预在激活空间中产生截然不同的几何分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否用更丰富的参数化方法（INLP）匹配差异均值法（DiM）在操控模型拒绝行为上的表现，并研究不同干预方法在激活空间中的几何特性差异。

Method: 在五个开源聊天模型上，比较DiM的激活添加和方向消融与INLP的零空间投影和反事实翻转四种干预方法，评估它们在抑制拒绝行为和保持困惑度方面的表现，并分析激活空间的几何分布。

Result: INLP反事实翻转在抑制拒绝上可媲美DiM方向消融，但零空间投影效果较弱；限制INLP到主要方向能在保持接近基线困惑度的情况下保留大部分抑制效果；零空间投影将激活压缩到有害与无害两个集群之间，而反事实翻转则将其移动到相反集群中。

Conclusion: 模型对概念缺失的编码方式与概念相反的编码方式存在本质区别，这一几何差异值得未来进一步研究。

Abstract: Arditi et al. (2024) has shown that refusal in safety fine-tuned chat models is mediated by a single linear direction in the residual stream, recoverable by a difference-in-means (DiM) of harmful and harmless activations. We compare DiM-based interventions (activation addition and directional ablation) with two interventions derived from Iterative Nullspace Projection (INLP) -- nullspace projection and counterfactual flipping -- on five open-weight chat models, asking whether INLP can match DiM at steering refusal and whether its richer parameterisation yields more tweakable interventions. INLP counterfactual flipping is competitive with DiM directional ablation on refusal suppression, while nullspace projection is consistently weaker. Restricting INLP to the leading directions of the extracted subspace preserves most of the suppression effect at near-baseline perplexity, giving a tunable capability. Geometrically, the two INLP interventions land in qualitatively different regions of activation space: nullspace projection collapses transformed activations \emph{between} the harmful and harmless clusters, while counterfactual flipping moves them into the opposite cluster, suggesting that the model encodes the absence of a concept differently from its opposite -- an intriguing distinction that warrants further investigation in future work.

</details>


### [126] [YeasierAgent: Agentic Social Sandbox as a Canvas for Intent-Driven Creation of Platform-Agnostic Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13722)
*Jory He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YeasierAgent提出了一种基于共生智能体、叙事世界和场景感知交互的应用构建范式，将应用重新定义为用户、智能体和世界之间的协作空间，实现了跨平台快速构建智能体原生应用，并统一了情感陪伴与实用工具执行功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统设备耦合的软件模型，将应用从固定的图形布局转变为用户、智能体与世界间的协作空间；解决现有智能体在情感陪伴与工具执行属性上的割裂问题。

Method: 提出平台无关的交互单元（智能体、场景、对话）取代固定图形布局；通过自动化生成、用户创建世界和空间多智能体协作实现共生智能体原生应用的形式化定义。

Result: 实现两大贡献：跨平台快速构建智能体原生应用；在单一体验沙盒中统一智能体的情感陪伴与实用工具执行属性。

Conclusion: YeasierAgent形式化了共生智能体原生应用这一类别，展示了从孤立的、特定工具的聊天机器人向有凝聚力的、社会嵌入的计算环境的转变。

Abstract: This paper introduces YeasierAgent, an application-building paradigm based on symbiotic agents, narrative worlds, and scene-aware interaction. It challenges the conventional device-coupled model of software by redefining applications as collaborative spaces among users, agents, and worlds. We present a system architecture that achieves two primary contributions: (1) enabling the rapid, cross-platform construction of agent-native applications by utilizing platform-agnostic interactive units (agents, scenes, dialogue) rather than fixed graphical layouts; and (2) unifying the emotional companionship and practical tool execution attributes of intelligent agents within a single experiential sandbox. By integrating automated generation, user-created worlds, and spatial multi-agent collaboration, YeasierAgent formalizes the category of Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications, demonstrating a shift from isolated, tool-specific chatbots toward cohesive, socially embedded computational environments.

</details>


### [127] [TwinBI: An Agentic Digital Twin for Efficient Augmented Interactions with Business Intelligence Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13731)
*Jisoo Jang Wen-Syan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinBI提出了一个智能体数字孪生框架，通过将LLM-based智能体系统与可执行的BI仪表板状态耦合，统一了对话交互、仪表板操作、语义基础和在多步分析中的溯源追踪，显著提升了分析准确性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BI系统在结合仪表板交互与LLM辅助时，用户在直接操作仪表板和自然语言查询之间切换，导致分析状态（如筛选器、层级、指标和图表上下文）难以保持一致，缺乏统一的交互状态管理机制。

Method: 提出TwinBI框架，核心是构建一个基于统一交互日志重建的可执行数字孪生仪表板状态，将LLM智能体系统与之耦合，实现对话交互、仪表板操作、语义基础和溯源追踪的统一管理，并暴露模式视图、SQL、日志和/insights命令等工件。

Result: 在A/B基准测试中，TwinBI将精确匹配准确率从43.3%提升至63.3%，部分匹配准确率从48.3%提升至70.8%，超时率从40.0%降至10.0%。可用性研究中，参与者在集成仪表板与聊天工作流中表现出高任务准确率、适度工作负荷和对状态感知交互机制的积极评价。

Conclusion: TwinBI通过将可视仪表板状态转换为更丰富的可操作上下文，同时提升了智能体级别的分析可靠性和面向用户的分析支持能力。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) increasingly combines dashboard interaction with LLM-based assistance, but these two modes often fall out of sync during multi-step analysis. As users switch between direct dashboard manipulation and natural-language queries, it becomes difficult to preserve a consistent analytical state across filters, hierarchies, metrics, and chart context. We present TwinBI, an agentic digital-twin framework that couples an LLM-based agent system with an executable BI dashboard state. TwinBI unifies conversational interaction, dashboard manipulation, semantic grounding, and provenance tracking through a shared analytical state reconstructed from a unified interaction log. It also exposes artifacts such as schema views, SQL, logs, and an /insights command for state-grounded analytical summaries. We evaluate TwinBI in two complementary ways. In a controlled A/B benchmark with the same backbone agent, TwinBI improves exact-match accuracy from 43.3% to 63.3%, partial-credit accuracy from 48.3% to 70.8%, and substantially reduces timeout rate from 40.0% to 10.0% relative to Dashboard alone. In a usability study, participants benefited from the integrated dashboard-and-chat workflow, with high task accuracy, moderate workload, and favorable ratings for state-aware interaction mechanisms. These results suggest that TwinBI improves both agent-level analytical reliability and user-facing analytical support by turning visible dashboard state into richer actionable context. Our dataset and source code are available at: https://github.com/simonjisu/TwinBI

</details>


### [128] [When Sample Selection Bias Precipitates Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13732)
*Xinbao Qiao,Xianglong Du,Wei Liu,Jingqi Zhang,Peihua Mai,Meng Zhang,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在低资源数据孤岛场景下，基于局部参考分布的数据选择反而会加速模型坍塌，而通过不共享原始数据的Wasserstein代理参考分布可缓解多样性退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归合成数据训练虽能缓解数据稀缺，但易导致模型坍塌（分布尾部被侵蚀、输出同质化）。数据选择被视为补救措施，但其可靠性依赖于验证器所使用的参考分布。在低资源验证场景（如医疗联盟或金融机构的私有数据孤岛）中，每个验证器只能观察到目标流形的碎片化、有偏子集，此时数据选择本身会引入偏差。

Method: 理论证明了孤岛式选择加速坍塌并导致幂律多样性衰减；提出了一种不共享原始数据的多孤岛Wasserstein代理参考构建方法作为初始缓解方案。

Result: 局部参考选择在有偏分布上失败，而协作式代理参考可缓解多样性退化。

Conclusion: 递归合成数据管道在真实数据覆盖碎片化或稀缺时需要特别谨慎，孤岛式数据选择会从防止坍塌的屏障转变为加速坍塌的机制，协作式代理参考提供了一种可行的缓解方向。

Abstract: The proliferation of recursive training on synthetic data can alleviate data scarcity but risks model collapse, where repeated training erodes distributional tails and homogenizes outputs. Data selection is widely viewed as a remedy, yet its reliability depends critically on the reference distribution used by the verifier. We show that in low-resource verification regimes, where each verifier observes only a small, fragmented, and biased slice of the target manifold, selection itself becomes biased. This situation naturally arises in low-resource data silos such as healthcare consortia or proprietary financial institutions, where raw data cannot be pooled and local references are inherently incomplete. As a result, selection preferentially retains samples aligned with the local manifold while pruning globally relevant tail modes, turning from a safeguard against collapse into a mechanism that precipitates it. We theoretically prove that such siloed selection accelerates collapse and induces power-law diversity decay. As an initial mitigation, we construct Wasserstein proxy references from multiple silos without sharing raw data. Empirical results confirm that local-reference selection fails on skewed distributions, whereas collaborative proxy references mitigate diversity degradation, suggesting that recursive synthetic-data pipelines require particular caution when real-data coverage is fragmented or scarce.

</details>


### [129] [AI Receptivity or AI Adoption Breadth? A Tool-Specific Reanalysis of the Lower-Literacy/Higher-Usage Link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13734)
*Hristo Inouzhe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文复现了Tully等人(2025)的研究，发现AI素养与AI使用之间的负相关关系具有异质性：仅存在于非文本AI工具中，且主要表现为采用/未采用的二元差异，而非使用频率的差异。这推翻了"低AI素养预测更高AI接受度"的简单论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并复现Tully, Longoni和Appel (2025)关于"较低AI素养预测更高AI接受度"的核心主张，检验该结论的稳健性与异质性。

Method: 使用Tully等人Study 3的公开数据，采用OLS、二元logit、有序logit和多项logit等多种规格，分别对聚合层面和按工具类型（文本AI与非文本AI）分层进行回归分析，并调整人口统计学协变量。

Result: 聚合层面复现了负相关关系，但分层后发现：AI素养对文本AI使用无显著预测作用（有序logit β=-0.090, p=.387），而对非文本AI使用有强显著负向预测（β=-0.377, p<.001）。进一步分析表明，该效应主要由采用/非采用差异驱动（调整后OR=0.68），而非使用频率差异。

Conclusion: 证据不支持"低AI素养预测更高AI通用接受度"的简单论断，而是指向更窄的模式：低AI素养者更可能采用渗透率较低的非文本AI工具。

Abstract: Recent evidence reported by Tully, Longoni, and Appel (2025) suggests that lower artificial intelligence (AI) literacy predicts greater receptivity toward AI. We revisit this claim using the public data from Study 3 of that article, which measures past usage of five AI tool categories on a five-point frequency scale. We first reproduce the negative association between AI literacy and aggregate AI usage using OLS on participant-level averages, binary logit, ordered logit, and multinomial logit specifications. We then show that the aggregate relationship masks substantial heterogeneity by tool type. In our demographic-adjusted primary specification, AI literacy does not significantly predict text AI usage (ordered-logit $β$ = -0.090, p = .387), whereas it remains a strong predictor of non-text AI adoption ($β$ = -0.377, p < .001). The non-text effect is also robust under Tully et al.'s original Study 3 control specification ($β$ = -0.502, p < .001). Binary, ordered-logit, and multinomial specifications suggest that the non-text relationship is primarily an adoption/non-adoption pattern rather than evidence of intensive use: the demographic-adjusted odds ratio of ever having used a non-text AI tool is 0.68. Thus, in the study that measures self-reported past usage rather than stated preferences, the evidence does not support a simple claim that lower AI literacy predicts greater receptivity to AI in general. It points instead to a narrower pattern of broader adoption across lower-penetration, non-text AI tools.

</details>


### [130] [MA-ProofBench: A Two-Tiered Evaluation of LLMs for Theorem Proving in Mathematical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13782)
*Lushi Pu,Weiming Zhang,Xinheng Xie,Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Hongya Lyu,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Yudong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MA-ProofBench，据我们所知，这是首个专门面向数学分析的形式化定理证明基准，包含200个覆盖6大核心主题和27个子类别的形式化定理，分为本科(Level I)和博士资格考试(Level II)两个难度等级。评估显示，最佳模型GPT-5.5在Level I上仅达16% Pass@8，Level II上仅5%，大多数模型在Level II上接近0%。主要失败模式为Mathlib幻觉和不完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明基准在数学覆盖范围和难度上存在局限，主要集中在代数、初等数论等易于形式化的领域，缺乏对数学分析等需要深度推理的子领域的覆盖。为弥补这一空白，亟需一个专门针对数学分析的形式化定理证明基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含200个形式化定理的基准MA-ProofBench，覆盖测度与积分理论、复分析、泛函分析等6大核心主题和27个子类别。问题分为本科水平(Level I, 100题)和博士资格考试水平(Level II, 100题)。每个问题通过人类主导、LLM辅助的形式化流程构建，并经过独立专家评审以确保忠实于原始数学。评估了多种通用推理模型和形式化定理证明器。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-5.5在Level I上Pass@8仅16%，Level II上仅5%；大多数模型在Level II上接近0%。错误分析识别出Mathlib幻觉和不完整证明是两大主要失败模式。自然语言版本评估显示非形式化推理与形式化推理之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: MA-ProofBench可作为追踪高级领域形式化数学推理进展的可靠基准，揭示了当前LLM在数学分析形式化证明方面的严重不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made notable progress in automated theorem proving, yet existing formal benchmarks remain limited in both mathematical coverage and difficulty. Most are concentrated in areas that are easier to formalize, such as algebra and elementary number theory, and provide limited coverage of subfields that require deeper reasoning, including mathematical analysis. To address this gap, we introduce MA-ProofBench, to the best of our knowledge, the first formal theorem-proving benchmark dedicated to Mathematical Analysis. The benchmark contains 200 formalized theorems covering 6 core topics and 27 subcategories, including measure and integration theory, complex analysis, and functional analysis. The problems are divided into two difficulty levels, an undergraduate level (Level I, 100 problems) and a Ph.D. qualifying level (Level II, 100 problems), to evaluate how well LLMs perform formal reasoning at different mathematical depths. Each problem is constructed through a human-led, LLM-assisted formalization pipeline followed by independent expert review, ensuring that the formal statements remain faithful to the original mathematics. We evaluate a range of recent general-purpose reasoning models and formal theorem provers on MA-ProofBench. However, most models perform poorly: even the best-performing model, GPT-5.5, achieves only 16% Pass@8 on Level I and 5% on Level II, while most models stay close to 0% on Level II. Further analysis identifies Mathlib hallucinations and incomplete proofs as the two dominant failure modes, while an evaluation on the natural-language version of the benchmark exposes a clear gap between informal and formal reasoning. MA-ProofBench is intended to serve as a reliable reference for tracking progress in formal mathematical reasoning in advanced domains.

</details>


### [131] [Poker Arena: Multi-Axis Profiling of Strategic Reasoning and Memory in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13815)
*Pratham Singla,Shivank Garg,Vihan Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Poker Arena平台，通过三层记忆架构和九轴认知剖面对前沿LLM在无损德州扑克中的战略推理能力进行多维评估，发现Claude Opus 4.6在筹码收益上领先但综合轴评分仅排第五，表明标量排行榜会系统性地错误排序模型能力结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博弈基准将异质推理维度压缩为单一标量，未能揭示前沿LLM的能力结构，因此需要更细粒度的多维评估框架。

Method: 构建Poker Arena平台，包含三层记忆架构（局内、会话内、跨会话）和九轴认知剖面的评估体系，在50轮每轮1000手的设置下评估七个前沿模型，并进行受控记忆消融实验。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6赢得+$15,730筹码和14次第一名，但在七模型中平均轴评分仅排第五；持久记忆对部分模型有帮助，对另一些则有损害。

Conclusion: 多轴评估揭示了标量排行榜系统性地错误排序的能力结构，跨维度一致性比单一维度的峰值表现更重要。

Abstract: Strategic reasoning under uncertainty underpins consequential decisions in negotiation, finance, and policy, but prevailing game-play benchmarks collapse heterogeneous reasoning dimensions into a single scalar, leaving the capability structure of frontier LLMs unexamined. We introduce Poker Arena, a no-limit Texas Hold'em tournament platform that couples a three-layer memory architecture (within-hand, session, and cross-session) with a nine-axis cognitive profile decomposing strategic reasoning into interpretable dimensions such as bet-sizing calibration and positional awareness. We evaluate seven frontier models across 50 sessions of 1,000 hands and a controlled memory ablation; tournament chips and aggregate axis score order the field differently: Claude Opus 4.6 wins +$15,730 chips with 14 first-place finishes, yet ranks only fifth of seven on mean axis score, while persistent memory helps some models and hurts others. These findings show that multi-axis evaluation surfaces capability structure that scalar leaderboards systematically misrank, with cross-dimensional consistency outweighing peak performance on any single axis.

</details>


### [132] [Hyperdimensional computing for structured querying on tabular data embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13871)
*Sebastián Bugedo,Stijn Vansummeren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用超维计算（HDC）中的全息简化表示（HRR）模型进行表格行嵌入，利用其代数性质推导出可解释的相似度阈值，在结构化查询检索任务中不仅匹配或优于现有方法EmbDI，还能可靠检测零匹配情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据嵌入方法（如EmbDI）缺乏可解释的相似度分数，无法设置原则性阈值来区分真匹配与"最不相似"的虚假匹配，尤其在零匹配检测场景中表现不佳，限制了实际部署。

Method: 采用超维计算（HDC）中的全息简化表示（HRR）模型进行表格行嵌入；利用HDC操作的代数性质推导等值谓词和非等值谓词的闭合形式期望相似度值，并据此确定合适的检索阈值。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，HDC在所有配置下匹配或优于EmbDI的行检索性能；非等值谓词处理更稳健；在足够维度下实现完美的属性投影精度；通过可解释阈值唯一实现了可靠的零匹配谓词识别。

Conclusion: HDC/HRR框架为表格行嵌入提供了一种具备可解释相似度分数的替代方案，有效解决零匹配检测问题，在结构化查询检索任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Tabular data embeddings have become a cornerstone of data profiling and data integration pipelines, enabling tasks such as entity annotation and resolution; schema matching; column type detection; and table search, among others. Existing approaches embed rows, columns, or entire tables into a vector space and rely on nearest-neighbor search to retrieve candidate matches. A fundamental limitation of current embedding methods is the lack of interpretable similarity scores: the concrete similarity value between a query and its nearest neighbour carries no intrinsic meaning, making it impossible to determine whether that neighbour is a true match or simply the least-dissimilar item in a corpus that contains no valid answer. This inability to set principled thresholds for retrieval undermines practical deployment, particularly for zero-match detection.
  We investigate the use of HyperDimensional Computing (HDC), specifically the Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) model, as a framework for tabular row embeddings when the retrieval task corresponds to answering structured select-project queries in vector space. Exploiting the algebraic properties of HDC operations, we derive closed-form expected similarity values for both equality and non-equality retrieval predicates, which converge to interpretable values as dimensionality increases, and use these to identify suitable retrieval thresholds. We evaluate HDC against EmbDI, a graph-based baseline, on two real-world datasets across varying table sizes and predicate lengths. Our results show that HDC matches or outperforms EmbDI for row retrieval across all configurations, handles non-equality predicates more robustly, and achieves perfect attribute projection accuracy at sufficient dimensionality -- while uniquely enabling reliable identification of zero-match predicates through its principled thresholds.

</details>


### [133] [Capability Minimization as a Safety Primitive: Risk-Aware Causal Gating for Least-Privilege LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13884)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知因果门控（RACG）框架，通过因果效应估计与校准风险控制相结合，决定是否采纳、延迟或拒绝模型预测，在保持效用的同时显著减少高成本错误，优于基于置信度和选择性预测的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代决策系统依赖的学习组件可能自信但错误，导致下游行动产生高昂代价。现有方法缺乏对预测因果风险的显式分离，无法在安全关键场景中可靠地控制风险。

Method: RACG结合因果效应估计与校准风险控制，建模从候选行动到结果的因果路径；导出无分布假设的高风险行动概率界，转化为满足用户安全约束的操作阈值；提出自适应门控策略，通过监测预测与实现结果之间的差异来应对分布偏移。

Result: 在模拟干预和真实决策基准上，RACG显著降低高成本错误，同时保持未门控策略的大部分效用；在匹配的弃权率下，优于基于置信度和选择性预测的基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式分离因果风险与预测不确定性可以构建更安全、更透明的决策系统，为高风险环境下的可信自动化提供原则性机制。

Abstract: Modern decision systems increasingly rely on learned components whose outputs may be confident yet wrong, exposing downstream actions to costly errors. We introduce Risk-Aware Causal Gating (RACG), a framework that decides whether to act on, defer, or abstain from a model's prediction by combining causal effect estimation with calibrated risk control. RACG models the causal pathway from candidate actions to outcomes and gates each decision according to an estimated counterfactual risk rather than raw predictive confidence. To make gating reliable, we derive distribution-free bounds on the probability of acting under high-risk conditions and show how these bounds translate into operating thresholds that satisfy user-specified safety constraints. We further propose an adaptive gating policy that adjusts to distribution shift by monitoring discrepancies between predicted and realized outcomes, tightening the gate when causal assumptions appear violated. Across simulated interventions and real-world decision benchmarks, RACG reduces high-cost errors substantially while preserving most of the utility of an ungated policy, and it outperforms confidence-based and selective-prediction baselines at matched abstention rates. Our results indicate that explicitly separating causal risk from predictive uncertainty yields decision systems that are both safer and more transparent, offering a principled mechanism for trustworthy automation in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [134] [A Multi-Agent AI System for Automated High School Transcript Processing: Collaborative Document Analysis at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13916)
*Ben Torkian,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体AI系统，通过三个专门智能体（模式识别、语义分析、视觉智能）协同工作，自动处理不同格式的高中成绩单，在40份真实成绩单上达到96.7%的准确率，每份处理时间45秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学招生办公室每年需要处理数百万份格式各异的高中成绩单，手动处理造成运营瓶颈，延迟录取决定并消耗大量资源。

Method: 构建多智能体架构，包括模式识别智能体（格式特定解析）、语义分析智能体（自然语言理解）和视觉智能智能体（多模态文档分析），由编排智能体协调通信和结果整合；关键创新在于以GPA提取作为协调信号进行基于智能体的质量控制。

Result: 在来自美国13个州40份真实成绩单上成功处理所有文档，准确率96.7%（与专家人工审核相比），处理速度为每份45秒。

Conclusion: 多智能体协调能有效解决复杂文档处理挑战，为机构提供可扩展的协作式AI解决方案，在保证准确性的同时大幅减少处理时间。

Abstract: Each year, college admissions offices face an overwhelming challenge: processing millions of high school transcripts, each with unique formats, grading systems, and layouts. This manual process creates operational bottlenecks that delay admissions decisions and consume valuable resources. We present a transformative solution through a multi-agent AI system where specialized agents collaborate to automatically process diverse transcript formats through intelligent coordination and communication. Our multi-agent architecture consists of three specialized agents-a Pattern Recognition Agent for format-specific parsing, a Semantic Analysis Agent for natural language understanding, and a Vision Intelligence Agent for multimodal document analysis-coordinated by an Orchestration Agent that manages agent communication and result reconciliation. Our key innovation lies in agent-based quality control using GPA extraction as a coordination signal, ensuring reliable agent collaboration and preventing critical information loss. When evaluated on 40 real world transcripts from high schools across 13 U.S. states, our agent system successfully processed every document, achieving 96.7% accuracy compared to expert manual review while maintaining practical processing speeds of 45 seconds per transcript. This work demonstrates how multi-agent coordination can solve complex document processing challenges, offering institutions a scalable, collaborative AI solution that preserves accuracy while dramatically reducing processing time.

</details>


### [135] [Sorries Are Not the Hard Part: An Expert-Review Case Study of a Semi-Autonomous Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13925)
*Vasily Ilin,Brian Nugent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Grothendieck消没定理的半自动化形式化案例研究表明，大语言模型能填补交互式定理证明器中的证明空缺，但生成的代码在定义选择、API设计等方面存在严重问题，无法通过专家评审。作者主张自动形式化的评估标准应从"无证明漏洞"转向"能否通过专家评审"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分"已验证的定理"与"可复用的库贡献"之间的差异，指出当前自动形式化评估只关注证明正确性而忽视代码质量，并提出应使用专家评审作为更严格的评估标准。

Method: 以Grothendieck消没定理的自动形式化为案例，先由大语言模型生成初始版本（编译通过），经专家评审发现问题后，进行评审驱动的重构与压缩，再接受第二轮专家评审，对比前后差异。

Result: 智能体在局部、机械可检查的反馈上适应良好，但在定义选择和API设计方面仍然薄弱。初始版本虽编译通过但存在严重问题，重构后虽有改进但仍不理想。

Conclusion: 自动形式化评估不应仅关注"证明无漏洞"，而应看最终形式化结果能否通过专家评审；当前的LLM在全局性、设计层面的推理能力仍有显著不足。

Abstract: Large language models can often close proof gaps in interactive theorem provers, but a verified theorem is not the same thing as a reusable library contribution. We study this distinction through a detailed case study: a semi-autonomous formalization of Grothendieck's vanishing theorem. The initial version compiles with no sorries, but an expert review found serious problems in definitions, theorem generality, file organization, and the API. We then ran a review-driven refactor and compression process and obtained a second expert review. The before-and-after comparison shows a sharp split: agents adapted well to local, mechanically checkable feedback, but remained weak at choosing definitions and designing APIs. We argue that autoformalization should be evaluated not only by closed sorries, but by whether the resulting formalization survives expert review.

</details>


### [136] [Adversarial Concept Search: Predicting Compositional Errors From Feature Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13934)
*Jennifer Meng Lu,Ruochen Zhang,Isabelle Lee,David Alvarez-Melis,Ellie Pavlick,Naomi Saphra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用LLM的表征几何结构预测其在组合任务上的失败模式，发现当概念编码近似正交时模型能成功组合，而当编码接近产生干扰时则失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类难以凭直觉判断哪些场景对LLM最具挑战性，传统方法依赖人工设计困难问题或构建大规模基准，缺乏可预测性。本文旨在通过模型的内部表征几何来主动预测其失败场景。

Method: 分析LLM的线性表征几何结构，测量概念对之间的编码角度（正交vs接近），以此预测模型在需要系统组合的任务（程序生成、多跳推理、多语言事实回忆）中的表现。

Result: 当概念编码接近正交时，模型能可靠组合；当编码接近（产生特征干扰）时，模型组合失败。该方法无需评估具体输入即可可靠预测失败模式。

Conclusion: 表征几何可用于识别高风险样例、构建针对性压力测试，并为实际部署中的主动学习提供可扩展基础。

Abstract: Humans cannot always intuit what scenarios are most challenging to LLMs. Hoping to capture challenging edge cases, developers either design problems to be difficult for humans or curate extensive benchmarks. What if we could instead anticipate which scenarios a model will fail on? In this paper, we use an LLM's representational geometry to predict which concept combinations it will fail on. We attribute this compositional failure to interference between salient features. In tasks that require systematic composition - toy programmatic settings, multihop reasoning, multilingual factual recall - we find that when a pair of concepts is encoded near-orthogonally, the model reliably composes them. When their linear encodings are close, producing interference, the model fails to compose them. Our method reliably anticipates failure modes across different compositional tasks, without evaluating specific inputs. These results lay the groundwork to use representational geometry to identify high-risk examples, construct targeted stress tests, and provide a scalable foundation for active learning in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [137] [Minim: Privacy-Aware Minimal View for Agents via Trusted Local Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13949)
*Hexuan Yu,Chaoyu Zhang,Heng Jin,Shanghao Shi,Ning Zhang,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MINIM是一个可信的本地代理，通过在客户端侧基于上下文完整性（CI）对UI元素进行双重评分（敏感度s和必要性n），执行三元披露策略（保留必要、抽象敏感、移除无关），在保持任务关键信息的同时大幅减少敏感数据的泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自主智能体在复杂数字环境中将完整UI状态传输到远程推理服务器，即使绝大多数元素与当前任务无关，这会导致认证码、私密通知、后台应用状态等敏感但非必要的上下文被泄露。

Method: 提出MINIM框架：1）在客户端侧部署一个可信本地代理；2）基于上下文完整性（CI）理论，为每个UI元素学习两个分数——固有敏感度（s）和任务条件必要性（n）；3）采用三元披露策略：保留必要元素、抽象敏感属性、移除任务无关内容；4）优化CI感知的目标函数，对高风险内容上的必要性错误施加更重惩罚。

Result: 在WebArena真实UI观察数据上的实验表明，MINIM在保持任务关键语义上下文和交互能力的同时，显著减少了任务无关的敏感信息泄露。

Conclusion: MINIM通过客户端侧隐私感知最小化，在不牺牲自主智能体任务性能的前提下，有效解决了UI状态传输中的敏感信息泄露问题，为安全可靠的LLM智能体部署提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM-powered autonomous agents increasingly rely on rich user interface (UI) state observations to achieve reliable action grounding in complex digital environments. However, many deployments transmit the full UI state to remote inference servers even when most elements are irrelevant to the current task, which can leak sensitive but unnecessary context such as authentication codes, private notifications, and background application states. We propose MINIM, a trusted local broker that performs privacy-aware minimization on the client side before any observation leaves the device. Grounded in Contextual Integrity (CI), MINIM learns a dual-score representation for each UI element by predicting an inherent sensitivity score (s) and a task-conditioned necessity score (n). These scores drive a ternary disclosure policy that keeps essential elements, abstracts sensitive attributes when needed, and removes task-irrelevant content. We optimize a CI-aware objective that penalizes necessity errors more strongly on high-risk content, enabling aggressive pruning while preserving task-critical information. Experiments on real-world UI observations derived from WebArena show that MINIM substantially reduces task-irrelevant sensitive leakage while preserving task-critical semantic context and the interactive affordances required for reliable agent actions.

</details>


### [138] [Formalizing Numerical Analysis: An Agent Pipeline and Quality Audit Beyond Kernel Acceptance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14000)
*Theodore Meek,Siyuan Ge,Di Qiu Xiang,Simon Chess,Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现有代码智能体形式化数学教材的局限性（集中于已完善支持的数学分支、仅以编译通过衡量成功），首次将编码智能体应用于数值分析教材的形式化，并提出了一个系统性、可复现的三维评估框架（语义正确性、Mathlib复用、跨文件复用），通过对比实验发现基于编译的指标严重高估了形式化质量，揭示了编译通过无法发现的多种不忠实形式化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化工作集中于mathlib中已完善支持的数学分支，且仅以编译通过（kernel acceptance）作为成功标准，忽略了形式化的语义忠实性。本文旨在解决这两个局限性，为自动形式化系统提供更严格的评估方法。

Method: 1) 将编码智能体应用于数值分析教材《常微分方程数值方法》的形式化（该领域在mathlib中几乎空白）；2) 提出三维评估框架：语义正确性、Mathlib复用度、跨文件复用度，采用LLM-as-judge方法；3) 将框架应用于自身形式化结果以及RepoProver和M2F的输出，进行系统比较分析。

Result: 发现了编译通过完全无法识别的多种不忠实形式化模式：不完整的多部分陈述、添加弱化假设、参数限制等。编译指标严重高估了形式化质量。

Conclusion: 基于编译的指标显著高估自动形式化质量，需要更严格的评估框架；本文提供了可复现的审计方法以支持未来自动形式化系统的更严谨评估。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated that coding agents can formalize entire advanced mathematics textbooks in Lean 4, yet existing efforts concentrate on branches of mathematics already well-represented in mathlib and measure success solely through kernel acceptance. We address both limitations by applying a coding agent to formalize Numerical Methods for Ordinary Differential Equations, a textbook in numerical analysis that is largely absent from mathlib, stressing the agent's capacity to develop new theory from scratch. We further introduce a systematic, reproducible three-dimensional framework for evaluating the quality of agent-produced formalizations beyond compilation: semantic correctness, Mathlib reuse, and cross-file reuse via LLM-as-judge methods. Applying this framework to our own formalization and to the released outputs of RepoProver and M2F, we uncover recurring unfaithful formalization patterns, including incomplete multi-part statements, added weakening hypotheses, and parameter restrictions, that kernel acceptance entirely obscures. Our results suggest that compilation-based metrics substantially overstate formalization quality, and we provide a reproducible audit methodology to support more rigorous evaluation of future autoformalization systems.

</details>


### [139] [Applicability Condition Extraction for Therapeutic Drug-Disease Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14031)
*Guanting Luo,Noriki Nishida,Yuji Matsumoto,Yuki Arase*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了药物-疾病适用性条件抽取任务，创建了首个包含药物、疾病和适用条件三元组的人工标注数据集（1,119个药物-疾病对），并提出了一种增强LoRA考虑药物-疾病关系的方法，该方法在多种评估设置下均优于强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学信息抽取方法大多只关注药物与疾病之间的关系识别，忽略了这些关系适用的上下文特定条件，如特定患者群体、用药时间、剂量等条件，而这些条件对于临床决策支持至关重要。

Method: 1) 创建了首个包含药物、疾病和适用条件三元组的人工标注数据集；2) 系统评估了多种现有方法在该任务上的性能；3) 提出了一种增强LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）的方法，使其能够显式考虑药物与疾病之间的关系信息。

Result: 提出的增强LoRA方法在多种评估设置下一致优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 本文引入的药物-疾病适用性条件抽取任务填补了现有研究的空白，所提出的方法和数据集为临床决策支持提供了有价值的资源，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Identifying conditions that a certain drug takes therapeutic effect on a target disease is crucial for clinical decision-making support. However, most existing biomedical information extraction methods have focused on identifying only relations between drugs and diseases, while largely overlooking the context-specific conditions where such relations can apply. To address this problem, we introduce the task of applicability condition extraction for therapeutic drug--disease relations from biomedical research literature. We create the first dataset that has manually annotated triples of drugs, diseases, and applicability conditions on biomedical paper abstracts with 1,119 drug-disease pairs. Using this dataset, we systematically evaluate the performance of a range of existing methods. In addition, we propose a new method that enhances LoRA to consider relations between drugs and diseases. Our method consistently outperforms strong baselines across different evaluation settings. The source code and dataset of this paper can be obtained from: https://github.com/guantingluo98/Drug-ACE

</details>


### [140] [FactoryLLM: A Safe and Open-Source AI Playground for Evaluating LLMs in Smart Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14119)
*Yash Pulse,Yong-Bin Kang,Abhik Banerjee,Abdur Forkan,Prem Prakash Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FactoryLLM，一个安全开源的AI平台，用于评估基于RAG的LLM在多机器文档推理中的故障诊断与恢复性能，通过双评估框架（RAGAS和LLM-as-a-Judge）实现，案例研究显示所有模型接地性得分均高于0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂中故障诊断与恢复面临挑战，关键信息分散在多台机器的互联文档中，亟需一种安全、可控的LLM评估平台来验证RAG模型在跨机器文档推理中的有效性。

Method: 提出FactoryLLM，一个可配置LLM、支持本地或开源模型的安全AI playground，采用双评估框架（RAGAS指标和NVIDIA的LLM-as-a-Judge指标）进行性能评估。通过自主智能车辆及其移动规划器软件的案例研究，对三个LLM在约600页跨机器文档的30个维护查询上进行评估。

Result: 所有模型在跨机器文档推理任务中接地性得分均超过0.88，验证了FactoryLLM在跨机器文档推理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FactoryLLM为智能工厂中基于RAG的LLM评估提供了安全、开源且有效的解决方案，代码和文档已公开，可供社区测试。

Abstract: Fault diagnostics and recovery in smart factories is challenging because critical information is dispersed across manuals of multiple machines which are interconnected through the manufacturing process. Large Language Models (LLMs) can provide a promising approach. In this paper, we propose FactoryLLM, a safe and open-source AI playground designed for evaluating different LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) models by analysing documents from multiple machines across the manufacturing process. FactoryLLM enables the user to configure the LLM, and assess performance when reasoning over multiple documents, through a dual evaluation setup using both RAGAS and NVIDIA's LLM-as-a-Judge metrics. FactoryLLM is safe because it allows users to run local or open-source LLMs without sharing sensitive industrial data, providing a controlled environment for experimentation. We demonstrate the efficacy of FactoryLLM through a case study which involves an Autonomous Intelligent Vehicle and its Mobile Planner software, evaluating three LLMs across 30 maintenance queries derived from approximately 600 pages of cross-machine documentation. The results suggest that FactoryLLM is effective in cross-machine document reasoning: every model achieved a groundedness score above 0.88. The full code and documentation for community to test FactoryLLM with their manufacturing specific scenarios are publicly available.

</details>


### [141] [VeriGeo: Controllable Geometry Question Generation with Numerical and Analytical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14176)
*Xiaoxian Duan,Zequn Liu,Yingce Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriGeo是一个可控的几何问题生成框架，通过可执行推理轨迹连接问题陈述、图表、约束和证明步骤，并利用三阶段验证管道（数值一致性、分析可实现性、全局一致性）确保生成质量。基于该框架生成的8.7k数据微调的多模态LLM在GeoQA上达到SOTA，并在PGPS9K和MathVista-GPS上取得强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何问题生成中，问题陈述、图表、约束和解决方案之间需要相互一致，但现有方法在可控性和可靠性之间存在权衡：基于种子重写的方法灵活但可验证性弱，基于图表优先的构建方法有效性高但难以满足任意用户指定的约束。

Method: 提出VeriGeo框架，包含：(1) Author agent根据用户约束（目标概念、难度等）生成问题与图表；(2) Solver agent生成与证明对齐的解决方案；(3) 共享动作序列连接自然语言、图表、几何约束和证明步骤；(4) 三阶段验证管道：数值一致性→分析可实现性→全局一致性，并通过验证引导的反射机制修复可恢复失败、拒绝不可恢复失败。

Result: 在五种LLM骨干上，原始生成频繁失败三阶段验证，VeriGeo能修复大量无效尝试。基于VeriGeo生成的8.7k示例进行监督微调，在GeoQA上达到多模态LLM求解器最佳报告性能，在PGPS9K和MathVista-GPS上取得强结果。

Conclusion: 验证后的合成数据能有效提升多模态几何推理能力，VeriGeo框架在可控性和可靠性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Geometry problem generation is useful for AI-assisted education and multimodal mathematical reasoning, but reliable synthesis remains difficult because the problem statement, diagram, constraints, and solution should be mutually consistent. Existing methods often trade off controllability and reliability: seed-based rewriting is flexible but weakly verifiable, whereas diagram-first construction improves validity but is less suited to arbitrary user-specified constraints. We introduce VeriGeo, a controllable geometry generation framework grounded in executable reasoning traces. Given user constraints such as target concepts and difficulty, an Author agent generates a problem and diagram, and a Solver agent produces a proof-aligned solution. Both agents use a shared action sequence that connects natural language, diagrams, geometric constraints, and proof steps into a verifiable representation. A three-stage pipeline checks numerical consistency, analytical realizability, and global consistency, using verification-guided reflection to repair recoverable failures and reject unrecoverable ones. Across five LLM backbones, raw generations frequently fail these checks, while VeriGeo repairs a substantial fraction of the invalid attempts. Supervised fine-tuning on 8.7k examples generated by VeriGeo achieves the best reported GeoQA performance among end-to-end multimodal LLM-based solvers, and obtains strong results on PGPS9K and MathVista-GPS, demonstrating the effectiveness of verified synthetic data for improving multimodal geometry reasoning.

</details>


### [142] [When Should Agent Trust Be Conditional? Characterizing and Attacking Skill-Conditional Reputation in Agent Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14200)
*Yihan Xia,Taotao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究开放平台中异构LLM智能体的技能条件信任问题，提出了技能条件信任框架R(i|k)替代传统全局信任评分。通过相图分析发现，条件信任仅在智能体高度异构、每技能证据稀疏且技能相关时有效；但跨技能证据借用存在安全隐患——攻击者可通过低成本技能的证据污染目标技能的路由决策。论文设计零成本条件信息值测试（CIVT）检测攻击，并量化了安全与效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放平台中LLM智能体在异构性（不同基座模型、脚手架、工具栈）下，各智能体的能力在不同技能维度上差异显著，传统单一全局信任评分无法捕捉这种专业化差异，导致路由决策无法充分利用智能体的专长。

Method: 采用相图分析（phase-diagram analysis）研究条件信任的适用条件；在14个异构AppWorld智能体基准上实证验证；提出Conditional Information Value Test (CIVT)检测攻击；构建攻击模型量化安全-效率权衡。

Result: 条件信任在高度异构、稀疏证据、技能相关的特定相区内有效；实际智能体池落在此有益相区内；但攻击者可通过跨技能证据借用实现路由劫持，将路由遗憾从0推至0.94；CIVT可检测攻击但无法完全消除，存在残余成本。

Conclusion: 技能条件信任能提升异构智能体路由效率，但跨技能证据借用是双刃剑——既是数据效率的来源，也是攻击的洗钱通道。论文不主张Sybil抗性，而是量化了安全与效率之间的根本权衡。

Abstract: Open platforms increasingly route tasks among heterogeneous LLM agents--differing in base model, scaffold, and tool stack--whose competence varies sharply by skill: an agent excellent at one skill may be useless at another. The standard reputation approach summarizes each agent by a single global trust score, but that scalar is the wrong object here, because routing every task to the globally most-trusted agent leaves the value of specialization unclaimed. We study skill-conditional trust R(i | k)--the trust to place in agent i for a task requiring skill k, rather than one score per agent--and pose three falsifiable questions: when is conditioning worth it, how much cross-skill evidence should be borrowed, and whether that borrowing is safe. A controlled phase-diagram analysis answers the first two: conditional trust wins only in a specific regime--high agent heterogeneity, sparse per-skill evidence, and correlated skills--and the coupling strength beta that buys this data efficiency is dual-use, because the same cross-skill borrowing is also a laundering channel. On a public benchmark of 14 genuinely heterogeneous AppWorld agents, real pools land inside the beneficial regime--a small but genuine gain, with the per-skill best agent genuinely changing across skills. We then show that an attacker with cheap evidence in one skill and none in a target skill hijacks the conditional router, driving routing regret from 0 to 0.94 on a pool our zero-cost Conditional Information Value Test (CIVT) rates GREEN--while the ungated trust verdict it contaminates reads -0.06 instead of the honest +0.19. A zero-evidence gate bounds the attack but does not eliminate it; we characterize the residual cost under an explicit budget. We do not claim Sybil-resistance--we quantify the trade-off.

</details>


### [143] [Closing the Reflection Gap: A Free Calibration Bonus for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14211)
*Yinglun Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM智能体存在“反思差距”——在观察到环境反馈后，即使对已正确回答的问题也会错误评估自身表现。提出RefGRPO方法，通过对比智能体自身反思与实际结果的自校准奖励和动态系数调度，同时提升反思校准能力和任务准确率（如text-to-SQL中，欠自信率从44.4%降至7.7%，准确率从75.1%提升至76.5%）。校准后的反思使智能体成为自身验证器，支持无结果监督的自我改进和更有效的测试时选择性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在与外部环境交互时，会观察到执行结果、错误信息和工具输出等反馈，但即使对已正确回答的问题，它们也倾向于错误评估自身输出，存在“反思差距”。标准强化学习（RL）由于信用分配不匹配，几乎无法缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出RefGRPO，在标准RL算法基础上增加两个关键组件：(1) 自由校准奖励——通过对比智能体自身的反思与实际结果来计算，无需额外奖励模型、LLM裁判或外部标注；(2) 对该系数的动态调度策略。

Result: 在text-to-SQL的五个基准上，相比标准RL基线，RefGRPO同时提升了反思校准能力（如欠自信率从44.4%降至7.7%）和任务准确率（如75.1%提升至76.5%）。校准后的反思使智能体能够：(i) 以反思作为伪奖励进行无结果监督的自我改进；(ii) 仅提交被标记为正确的输出来实现更有效的测试时选择性预测。

Conclusion: 通过对比反思与实际结果的自校准机制可有效弥合LLM智能体的反思差距，使智能体成为基于环境反馈的自身验证器，从而提升任务性能和自我改进能力。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as agents that interact with external environments and observe feedback such as execution results, error messages, and tool outputs. A well-functioning agent should be able to leverage this feedback to accurately assess its own performance. Yet we find a persistent reflection gap: LLM agents tend to mis-assess their own outputs after observing concrete environment feedback -- even for questions they correctly answered -- and standard RL barely helps due to a credit-assignment mismatch. To close this gap, we propose RefGRPO, a simple yet effective fix that augments standard RL algorithms with two key ingredients: a free calibration bonus computed by contrasting the agent's own reflection with the actual outcome (requiring no additional reward model, LLM judge, or external annotation), and a dynamic schedule on its coefficient. Compared to standard RL baselines, our method simultaneously improves reflection calibration (e.g., reduces underconfidence rate $44.4\% \to 7.7\%$) and task accuracy (e.g., $75.1\% \to 76.5\%$) on text-to-SQL across five benchmarks. The resulting calibrated reflection turns the agent into its own verifier grounded in environment feedback, which further enables (i) better self-improvement that uses reflections as pseudo-rewards without outcome supervision, and (ii) more effective test-time selective prediction by committing only to rollouts flagged as correct.

</details>


### [144] [SkillAudit: Ground-Truth-Free Skill Evolution via Paired Trajectory Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14239)
*Haowen Gao,Haoran Chen,Can Wang,Shasha Guo,Liang Pang,Zhaoyang Liu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillAudit 提出了一种无需真值反馈即可演化智能体技能的方法，通过配对轨迹审计（同时执行有/无技能的同一任务）和流程对齐对比评估（PACE）将行为差异映射为技能的编辑信号，在89个任务上达到73.9%的平均奖励，显著优于无技能基线（40.9%）和静态专家技能（56.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能在部署后常常因边缘情况、API变更和部署约束而失效，但现有技能演化方法依赖保留验证集、隐藏测试结果或环境奖励等特权反馈信号，而实际场景中从业者往往只有任务描述和工作区数据，缺乏这类真值反馈信号。

Method: SkillAudit 的核心是配对轨迹审计：每轮迭代中对同一任务分别执行有技能和无技能版本，隔离技能对行为的改变；使用流程对齐对比评估（PACE）将轨迹分歧映射到技能文档特定段落的诊断信号；通过从任务规范编译的结构化验证器检查约束并回滚有害更新；编辑分为 Refine（移除噪声/无关指引）和 Repair（替换与任务冲突的段落）两条管线。

Result: 在覆盖8个专业领域的89个容器化任务上，SkillAudit 达到73.9%的平均任务奖励，对比无技能基线40.9%、静态专家技能56.7%，且演化过程中无需访问隐藏测试、参考答案或外部评分函数。

Conclusion: SkillAudit 证明在缺乏真值反馈的情况下，通过配对审计和行为差异对比可以有效演化智能体技能，为实际部署中的技能维护提供了一种可行的无监督方案。

Abstract: Agent skills are structured procedural packages that guide frozen LLM agents in specialized workflows. Skills rarely remain sufficient after deployment: edge cases, API changes, and deployment constraints become visible only through use, making skill evolution a practical necessity. Existing methods depend on privileged feedback such as held-out validation scores, hidden test outcomes, or environment rewards -- signals often unavailable when a practitioner has only a task description and workspace data. We introduce SkillAudit, a framework for evolving agent skills without ground-truth feedback. The key idea is paired trajectory auditing: at each iteration, the same task is executed with and without the candidate skill, isolating how the skill changes agent behavior without external labels. To turn behavioral differences into edit guidance, SkillAudit uses Process-Aligned Contrastive Evaluation (PACE), a cluster of evaluators that maps trajectory divergences to diagnostic signals linked to specific passages in the skill document. A structural verifier, compiled once from the task specification and then fixed, checks task constraints and rolls back harmful updates. SkillAudit routes edits through two pipelines: Refine removes noisy or irrelevant guidance from broadly useful skills, while Repair replaces passages that conflict with the task. Across 89 containerized tasks spanning 8 professional domains, SkillAudit achieves 73.9% average task reward, outperforming an agent without skills (40.9%) and the static expert skill (56.7%). These gains are obtained without accessing hidden tests, reference solutions, or external scoring functions during evolution.

</details>


### [145] [AFFORDANCE20Q: Evaluating Affordance Reasoning from Physical Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14240)
*Yifan Jiang,Meige Yang,Zitong Li,Jay Pujara*

Main category: cs.AI
